src/map.rs SourceMap envelope (version/file/source_root/sources/sources_content/names/mappings/ignore_list); SourceMap::v3() ctor pins version=3
src/index.rs SourceIdx(u32), NameIdx(u32) newtypes; OriginalLocation
src/error.rs ModelError{ FieldCount(usize), SourceOob{idx,len}, NameOob{idx,len}, UnsortedColumn{line,col}, BadVersion(u8) }
  • Segment always stores 5 fields physically; source/name are Option<u32> → distinguishes shape 1 (both None) vs 4 (source Some, name None) vs 5 (both Some) (per INV-4, §8.2).
  • Invariant: source.is_none() ⟺ shape 1 (generated-only); name.is_some() ⟹ source.is_some() (a name field requires the 4 preceding fields). Constructor rejects name-without-source (per §6.15, INV-4).
  • All lines/columns 0-based in model; 1-based only at the CLI/API boundary — ONE conversion seam (per §6.4, MM5, ADR-009). The grammar.md defines the reset rules: gen_column resets per ;, the other four fields carry across (per §6.3, INV-3).
  • DecodedMap::validate() walks each inner Vec: asserts non-decreasing gen_column (per INV-12), all source<sources.len(), all name<names.len() (per INV-5; cross-checked against a SourceMap via validate_against).

2.3 Deliverable

smap-model crate compiles (no_std + alloc); all five core types + newtypes + ModelError present with constructors and validate()/validate_against(). A documented grammar.md (EBNF + reset-rule table) ships in the crate. Unit-test suite covers shape construction + malformed rejection. cargo test -p smap-model green.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Segment::with_name on a None-source builder path is a compile error or returns ModelError::FieldCount — name-without-source impossible to construct.
  • DecodedMap::validate() returns Err(UnsortedColumn) for any inner Vec with a descending gen_column; Ok otherwise.
  • SourceMap::v3() yields version==3; constructing with any other version is unrepresentable (no public setter).
  • Round-trip a hand-built 3-line DecodedMap (shapes 1,4,5 present) through validate() → Ok, with iter_segments count == sum of inner lens.
  • EBNF in grammar.md parses the canonical example AAAA; / AAgBC examples by hand-trace (documented, not executed here).

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
model/segment_shape1.json 1-field seg SegmentShape::Generated, source=None, name=None
model/segment_shape4.json 4-field seg Mapped; name=None
model/segment_shape5.json 5-field seg Named; both Some
model/sorted_line.json gen_cols [0,5,12] validate() Ok
model/unsorted_line.json (malformed) gen_cols [5,0] Err(UnsortedColumn{line:0,col:0})
model/name_oob.json (malformed) name idx 9, names.len 2 Err(NameOob{idx:9,len:2})
model/bad_fieldcount.json (malformed) 3-field seg Err(FieldCount(3))
model/source_oob.json (malformed) source idx 4, sources.len 1 Err(SourceOob{idx:4,len:1})

2.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
Treating field-count 2/3 as valid Spec allows ONLY 1/4/5; 2/3 are malformed Assert in SegmentShape::from_count; fuzz rejects 0,2,3,6
Storing name on a generated-only seg Confusing Option layering Enforce name⟹source invariant in ctor, not encode
1-based leakage into model Devtools show 1-based lines; tempting to store as-is Grep model crate for +1/-1; must be zero — boundary is API/CLI only
Sorting whole map by gen_column globally Sort is per generated line, not global; outer index already encodes line Sort inner Vecs only; never reorder outer Vec
Assuming carried deltas live in the model Reset/carry semantics belong to codec state (Phase 3); model holds ABSOLUTE values Model Segment fields are absolute, not deltas — document loudly

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • Segment = 5×u32 + 2×discriminant-folded-into-Option → keep ≤ 24 bytes; verify size_of::<Segment>() ≤ 24 via static assert (Option<u32> niche-optimized to 8 bytes? no — use sentinel u32::MAX internally if needed; documented decision deferred to Phase 12).
  • DecodedMap is Vec<Vec<Segment>>: outer realloc cost amortized; reserve per-line in builder (Phase 5). No allocation in validate() (single pass, O(total segments)).
  • Newtypes SourceIdx/NameIdx are #[repr(transparent)] u32 — zero runtime cost.

2.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty DecodedMap (Vec len 0) → valid (empty map, mappings == "").
  • Generated line with zero segments → valid inner empty Vec → encodes to bare ;.
  • Trailing empty lines → preserved (semicolon count is significant; do not trim).
  • gen_column == u32::MAX and duplicate gen_column on same line → allowed (ties; not strictly ascending, only non-decreasing).
  • sources entry None (null source) and sources_content shorter than sources → both legal per v3; model permits, does not pad.

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
Malformed shape constructed in-memory validate() / ctor guard Err(ModelError::*), no panic
Index OOB vs envelope validate_against(&SourceMap) Err(SourceOob/NameOob) with offending idx+len
Wrong version deserialized later (Phase 4) SourceMap::v3 invariant rejected at JSON boundary, not model
Unsorted columns slip past builder validate() pre-encode in Phase 3 block encode; surface line/col

All model APIs are total/Result-returning; smap-model contains zero panic!/unwrap on data paths.

2.10 Operational Notes

  • grammar.md is the single source of truth for the mappings grammar; Phase 3 codec MUST cite it and not redefine reset rules.
  • Reset-rule contract (carried to codec, restated here once):
field 1 gen_column  : reset to 0 at every ";"  (per generated line)
field 2 source idx  : carry ACROSS lines
field 3 orig_line   : carry ACROSS lines
field 4 orig_column : carry ACROSS lines
field 5 name idx    : carry ACROSS lines
  • EBNF (informative; Phase 3 owns the encoder/decoder):
mappings = group ( ";" group )* ;
group    = [ segment ( "," segment )* ] ;
segment  = field | field field field field | field field field field field ;
field    = vlq ;                 (* one Base64-VLQ integer *)
  • 0-based internal; document the boundary in crate-level //! docs so downstream smap-consumer/smap-cli authors convert exactly once.

15. Phase 3 — Mappings codec (encode/decode state machine)

Owner: Mujir Qureshi. Crate: smap-codec (no_std + alloc). Depends: smap-vlq, smap-model. Files (per §10): crates/smap-codec/src/lib.rs, encode.rs, decode.rs, state.rs, error.rs. Surface: encode_mappings, decode_mappings, CodecError, internal EncoderState/DecoderState.

3.1 Objectives

  • encode_mappings(&DecodedMap) -> String: walk lines, emit ; per line boundary, , between segments, 1/4/5 VLQ fields as running deltas.
  • decode_mappings(&str) -> Result<DecodedMap, CodecError>: split on ; → generated lines; per ,-segment read 1/4/5 fields, fold into running state, validate.
  • Enforce reset rules: gen_column delta resets to 0 per ;; source/orig_line/orig_column/name carry ACROSS lines.
  • Tolerant decode of real-world maps: trailing ;/,, empty groups, leading ;.
  • Round-trip stable: decode→encode→decode fixpoint over fixtures + fuzz. Never panic.

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File Contents
state.rs struct EncoderState { prev_gen_col:u32, prev_source:u32, prev_orig_line:u32, prev_orig_col:u32, prev_name:u32 }; DecoderState mirror (i64 accumulators). reset_line(&mut self) zeroes only prev_gen_col — the asymmetric reset that is INV-3 / MM2.
encode.rs encode_mappings: for (li,line) in decoded.0.iter().enumerate(): if li>0 push ; + state.reset_line() (per INV-3); for (si,seg) in line push , if si>0; emit field1 = seg.gen_column as i64 - prev_gen_col; if seg.source.is_some() emit fields 2–4 (source,orig_line,orig_column deltas); if seg.name.is_some() emit field 5; update all prev_*. Each field via encode_vlq(delta, &mut out). Output buffer pre-sized to avoid reallocation (per §6.9, C9).
decode.rs decode_mappings: byte cursor over &[u8]; segment loop reads VLQ fields into a [i64;5] buffer counting n; on ,/;/EOF finalize: `match n {1
error.rs enum CodecError { BadFieldCount(usize), Vlq(VlqError), NegativeColumn, SourceIndexOob } + From<VlqError>.
lib.rs re-export public fns/types; #![no_std], extern crate alloc.
  • Field-count validation: only {1,4,5} legal; n∈{2,3} → BadFieldCount. Empty segment (n=0 between ,,) tolerated → skipped.
  • Negative-result accumulators: after add, any of gen_col/orig_line/orig_col < 0 → NegativeColumn (name/source <0 also rejected here).
  • decode_mappings does NOT have sources.len(); SourceIndexOob surfaces only when codec is given bounds (consumer/builder pass them) — internal decode records raw index, OOB checked at SourceMapConsumer::new.

3.3 Deliverable

smap-codec crate: encode_mappings + decode_mappings + CodecError, no_std, zero panics on arbitrary &str. cargo test -p smap-codec green; property test decode→encode→decode fixpoint passes; round-trips every Phase-3 fixture byte-identically after one normalization pass.

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Encode of hand-authored DecodedMap matches golden mappings string byte-for-byte.
  • Decode of all valid fixtures yields expected Vec<Vec<Segment>> (assert exact Segment fields).
  • Idempotence: encode(decode(encode(decode(s)))) == encode(decode(s)) for all fixtures + 10k fuzz inputs.
  • Every malformed fixture returns the documented CodecError variant (not panic, not wrong variant).
  • cargo +nightly miri test -p smap-codec clean (no UB in cursor arithmetic).

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Input Expected
valid_one_field.txt AAAA then ;A line0: 1 seg n=4; line1: 1 seg n=1 (gen-only)
valid_five_field.txt AAAAA,CAEKA 2 segs, name index present, deltas applied
multiline.txt AAgBC;;AAACA line0 seg, line1 empty [], line2 carries source/orig across blank line
empty_groups.txt ;;; 4 lines, all []
trailing_sep.txt AAAA,; line0 1 seg + tolerated trailing ,; line1 []
bad_fieldcount.txt AA (n=2) Err(BadFieldCount(2))
negative_col.txt seg forcing gen_col→-1 Err(NegativeColumn)
bad_char.txt A!A Err(Vlq(InvalidBase64Char(b'!')))
overflow.txt 11×g+A Err(Vlq(Overflow))

Golden round-trip corpus: re-encode of @jridgewell/sourcemap-codec test vectors + a real react-dom.min.js.map mappings field.

3.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
gen_column not reset per line forgot reset_line() on ;; only prev_gen_col resets, NOT source/orig/name dump deltas at each ;; col explodes on line 2
Resetting source/orig/name on ; over-zealous reset — those carry ACROSS lines second-line origins land at wrong file/line
Empty trailing segment emitted encoder pushes , before checking segment exists re-encode adds stray ,; idempotence fails
Sign vs two's-complement confusion VLQ is sign-bit+magnitude; negative deltas common (col back-references) decode of negative col yields huge positive
n∈{2,3} silently accepted missing field-count guard wrong Segment shape downstream; assert n∈{0,1,4,5}
Off-by-one cursor on multibyte boundary decode_vlq must advance pos past every consumed char incl. last group infinite loop / EOF; assert pos monotonic

3.7 Performance Concerns

  • Decode is byte-at-a-time over &[u8]; no per-char String alloc. Pre-size outer Vec to bytecount(b';')+1 lines; reserve inner Vec<Segment> ~ avg segs/line.
  • Encoder writes into one reused String with out.reserve(decoded segments * ~6); no intermediate Vec<u8> per field.
  • Target ≥150 MB/s decode, ≥120 MB/s encode single-thread (full budget in Phase 12). No bounds-check in hot loop beyond slice indexing the reverse-Base64 table.

3.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty mappings string "" → DecodedMap(vec![vec![]]) (one empty line) by convention; document.
  • Only ; separators (no segments) → N empty lines.
  • Leading ; → first generated line is [].
  • 1-field segment after 4/5-field: gen-only segment legal; source/orig state untouched but NOT emitted.
  • Max VLQ: i32 column range; reject i64 overflow via VlqError::Overflow.
  • Last line without trailing ; (normal) vs accidental trailing ; (tolerated empty final line) — both round-trip after normalization.

3.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Malformed VLQ char reverse table = sentinel Err(Vlq(InvalidBase64Char)), cursor at offending byte
Truncated continuation (EOF mid-group) continuation bit set at slice end Err(Vlq(UnexpectedEof))
Delta drives field negative post-accumulate sign check Err(NegativeColumn); no partial DecodedMap returned
Illegal field count n after segment terminator Err(BadFieldCount(n)) with actual count
Source index OOB deferred to consumer with sources.len() Err(SourceIndexOob) at SourceMapConsumer::new, not here

Invariant: decode_mappings returns Ok xor Err, never partial state, never panic — fuzz-enforced (Phase 11).

3.10 Operational Notes

  • smap-codec is the choke point for both smap-builder::into_sourcemap (encode) and smap-consumer::new (decode); a codec regression breaks every downstream crate — gate CI on the idempotence property.
  • Logging: codec is no_std/silent; surface byte-offset of first error in CodecError (caller maps to smap-cli diagnostic). 0-based throughout; 1-based conversion lives only at CLI/API boundary, never here.
  • Versioning: mappings grammar is frozen by Source Map v3; any new field count (>5) is a spec change, not a patch — gate behind explicit feature, default reject.

16. Phase 4 — v3 JSON serialization, sourceMappingURL, and inline maps

4.1 Objectives

  • Implement smap-json (std): to_json, from_json, extract_source_mapping_url — the wire/disk boundary between SourceMap (smap-model) and the v3 JSON envelope.
  • from_json → parse v3 JSON, strip XSSI )]}' line + optional UTF-8 BOM, validate version==3, read all fields, zero-copy borrow sources/names where the parser allows.
  • to_json → emit canonical, deterministic v3 envelope (stable key order, valid JSON string escaping, version:3 always first).
  • extract_source_mapping_url → find trailing //# sourceMappingURL= / //@ comment; decode data:application/json;base64, inline → SourceMapRef::Inline; else SourceMapRef::Url.
  • NON-GOAL: re-encoding mappings (Phase 3 smap-codec); query/index build (Phase 6). smap-json treats mappings as an opaque String.

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File Adds
smap-json/src/lib.rs re-exports to_json, from_json, extract_source_mapping_url, SourceMapRef, JsonError
smap-json/src/parse.rs from_json(text:&str)->Result<SourceMap,JsonError>; envelope reader
smap-json/src/emit.rs to_json(map:&SourceMap)->String; canonical writer + string-escape
smap-json/src/url.rs extract_source_mapping_url(code:&str)->Option<SourceMapRef>; data:-URL base64 decode
smap-json/src/prelude.rs XSSI/BOM strip helpers, strip_prefixes(&str)->&str
  • JSON backend: serde_json with RawValue for mappings/sourcesContent strings (avoid re-alloc), OR a hand-rolled struct deserializer; either way mappings lands as one owned String, no per-segment work. Unknown top-level keys silently ignored — forward-compat policy (per §6.7, ADR-015, Kleppmann §5.2).
  • from_json order: (1) strip_prefixes removes BOM \u{FEFF} then a leading )]}'...\n line (per ADR-016); (2) parse object; (3) read version → JsonError::BadVersion if !=3; (4) mappings required → MissingField("mappings"); (5) optional file/sourceRoot/sources/sourcesContent/names/ignoreList (alias x_google_ignoreList); defaults: missing arrays → empty Vec. Never panics on any &str (per §6.2, INV-10).
  • sources/sourcesContent are Vec<Option<String>> → JSON null ⇒ None; length mismatch (sourcesContent.len() != sources.len()) tolerated, pad with None (per §6.7, ADR-017).
  • to_json writes keys in fixed order: version, file?, sourceRoot?, sources, sourcesContent?, names, mappings, x_google_ignoreList?; omit None/empty ignoreList; emit sources null for None entries; escape per RFC 8259. Fixed key order ensures determinism (per §6.11, ADR-011).
  • extract_source_mapping_url: scan from EOF backward for last non-blank line; match ^\s*//[#@]\s*sourceMappingURL=(\S+)\s*$ (and /*...*/ block form); URL → if data: prefix, split on first ,, require ;base64 token, base64-decode payload, feed to from_json → Inline; else Url(trimmed). Library never fetches; returns the reference only (per §25 T6–T7, MM8).

4.3 Deliverable

smap-json compiles (std); to_json(from_json(x)) round-trips a corpus of real tsc/esbuild/terser/@jridgewell/gen-mapping maps byte-stable after one normalization pass; extract_source_mapping_url resolves both external Url and inline data: maps; smap-cli extract-url <file> prints the resolved ref.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Round-trip: for ≥30 real maps, from_json succeeds and to_json∘from_json∘to_json is byte-identical to to_json∘from_json (idempotent after first canonicalization).
  • version != 3 ⇒ Err(BadVersion(n)); missing mappings ⇒ Err(MissingField("mappings")); truncated JSON ⇒ Err(Parse(..)). Never panics on any &str.
  • XSSI )]}' prefix and leading BOM both stripped; absence of either still parses.
  • extract_source_mapping_url: external URL, inline data:application/json;base64,, //@ legacy form, and "no comment" → correct Some(Url)/Some(Inline)/None.
  • sources containing null survives round-trip as JSON null (not "").

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
fixtures/json/tsc.basic.map parses; version==3; sources.len()==sourcesContent.len() after pad; round-trips
fixtures/json/esbuild.min.map names non-empty; sourceRoot==None; idempotent re-emit
fixtures/json/terser.ignorelist.map x_google_ignoreList → ignore_list==[..]
fixtures/json/xssi-prefixed.map leading )]}'\n stripped → parses
fixtures/json/bom.map leading ﻿ stripped → parses
fixtures/json/null-source.map sources:[null,"a.ts"] ⇒ [None,Some("a.ts")] round-trips
fixtures/url/external.js trailing //# sourceMappingURL=out.js.map ⇒ Url("out.js.map")
fixtures/url/inline.js data:application/json;base64,.. ⇒ Inline(SourceMap{version:3,..})
fixtures/url/legacy-at.js //@ sourceMappingURL= ⇒ Url(..)
Malformed version:2→BadVersion(2); {}(no mappings)→MissingField; {"version":3,"mappings": (truncated)→Parse; data:text/plain,xx (no base64)→None/Parse; non-UTF8 base64 payload→Parse

4.6 Pitfalls

  • )]}' stripped naively as 4 bytes → breaks when prefix is )]}'\n vs )]} variants or has trailing junk on the line. Root cause: spec says strip to the first newline. Strip the whole first line if it starts with )]}. Debug: hexdump first 8 bytes.
  • BOM only at byte 0 → a BOM after the XSSI line is not a BOM; strip BOM before XSSI, once. Debug: assert !text.starts_with('\u{FEFF}') post-strip.
  • sources/sourcesContent length skew → real tsc maps sometimes omit trailing sourcesContent; indexing later panics. Root cause: assuming equal length. Pad sourcesContent to sources.len() with None.
  • null vs empty string in sources → emitting "" for None corrupts path resolution downstream. Preserve JSON null. Debug: grep emitted JSON for "sources":["".
  • sourceMappingURL inside a string literal / mid-file → matching anywhere yields false positives. Root cause: scanning whole file. Only the last matching comment line counts. Debug: log byte offset of match.
  • base64 with URL-safe alphabet or missing ;base64 → data:...;charset=utf-8;base64, and data:application/json,<urlencoded> both occur. Root cause: assuming one form. Branch on ;base64 token presence; non-base64 ⇒ percent-decode path or None.

4.7 Performance Concerns

  • mappings (largest field, MBs) must be borrowed/moved once, never copied per-char — use RawValue or a single String::from. Target: from_json allocates O(#sources + #names + 1) strings, not O(map size).
  • to_json pre-sizes output String to mappings.len() + Σsources + Σcontent + slack to avoid reallocation; single pass, no intermediate serde_json::Value tree.
  • extract_source_mapping_url scans backward from EOF (last ~256 bytes typical), not full-file forward → O(1) amortized for large bundles.
  • Zero-copy borrow of sources/names when caller owns the buffer; deserialized SourceMap owns Strings (no lifetime escape) — borrow is an internal parse optimization only.

4.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty map: {"version":3,"sources":[],"names":[],"mappings":""} → valid, round-trips.
  • mappings:"" (no segments) and whitespace-only file for URL extraction → None.
  • Duplicate keys in JSON object → last-wins (serde default); document, don't error.
  • version as JSON string "3" (some legacy emitters) → coerce or BadVersion; choose strict BadVersion and note tolerant flag.
  • sourceRoot with trailing / and absolute/relative forms preserved verbatim (no normalization here — that is Phase 10).
  • Inline data: URL whose decoded JSON is itself an index map → from_json rejects (no sections support); return Parse (index maps are Phase 8 / separate entry point).
  • CRLF line endings before the //# comment → trim \r.

4.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Cause Mitigation
BadVersion(n) non-3 envelope reject; never silently upconvert
MissingField("mappings") absent/null mappings hard error; mappings is mandatory
Parse(msg) malformed JSON / bad base64 / non-UTF8 wrap underlying error msg; no panic
silent path corruption None→"" coercion preserve null (4.6)
OOM on hostile input giant sources/duplicated keys size cap (JsonError::Parse("too large")) at configurable ceiling
false URL match comment-like string in code last-line-only match (4.6)

4.10 Operational Notes

  • smap-cli inspect <map> → from_json then summary (version, #sources, #names, mappings len, has ignoreList); extract-url <file> → print Url/Inline kind + decoded summary.
  • to_json output is byte-stable across runs (deterministic key order) → safe for content-addressed caching / build reproducibility.
  • All JsonError carry actionable context (field name, byte offset where available); CLI exit code 2 for parse errors, 0 on success.
  • Feature flag tolerant relaxes version-string coercion and length skew for ingesting third-party maps; default strict for self-emitted maps.
  • Fuzz target (fuzz_json) wired here, exercised fully in Phase 11: arbitrary &[u8] → from_json must return Err, never panic/UB.

17. Phase 5 — The generator/builder API (SourceMapGenerator)

smap-builder: the contract a transpiler/minifier/bundler builds against while emitting code. Accepts add_mapping calls in arbitrary order, interns sources+names, and on into_sourcemap sorts/dedups/validates and delegates to smap-codec::encode_mappings. Models Mozilla SourceMapGenerator + @jridgewell/gen-mapping.

5.1 Objectives

  • SourceMapBuilder: stateful accumulator → spec-valid SourceMap (version 3).
  • add_source/add_name: dedup interning → stable first-seen indices; add_source returns u32 source index, add_name returns u32 name index.
  • set_source_content(source, content): index-aligned with sources (sparse → None fill).
  • add_mapping(gen, Option<(u32, Position)>, Option<u32>): buffer one Segment on gen.line; order-independent.
  • into_sourcemap: per-line sort by gen_column, exact-dup elision, monotonic-ordering validation, encode mappings.

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files: smap-builder/src/lib.rs, intern.rs (interner), builder.rs (SourceMapBuilder), validate.rs (ordering checks). Deps: smap-model, smap-codec. no_std + alloc.

pub struct SourceMapBuilder {
    file: Option<String>, source_root: Option<String>,
    sources: Interner,                      // String -> u32, insertion-ordered
    names: Interner,
    sources_content: Vec<Option<String>>,   // index-aligned to sources
    lines: Vec<Vec<Segment>>,               // outer = gen line (0-based), grown lazily
    ignore_list: Vec<u32>,
}
  • Interner = Vec<String> (index→string) + HashMap<String,u32> (string→index, or BTreeMap under no_std/alloc). intern(&str) -> u32: return existing or push. Insertion order determines output index — no hash-iteration nondeterminism (per §6.11, INV-13, ADR-011). Sources allow None slots (skipped-source maps).
  • add_mapping: ensure lines.len() > gen.line (extend w/ empty Vec); push Segment{ gen_column: gen.column, source/orig_line/orig_column from the Some arm or sentinel, name }. Generated-only mapping (orig = None) → 1-field segment (source/name forced None) — null-segment model (per §6.6, INV-8).
  • into_sourcemap: for each line sort_unstable_by_key(|s| s.gen_column) → stable tie-break by insertion index (use sort_by w/ secondary key, NOT sort_unstable, to keep last-writer-wins deterministic) (per §6.11, ADR-010); dedup exact-equal adjacent segments; validate.rs asserts non-decreasing gen_column (per INV-12); build DecodedMap; mappings = encode_mappings(&decoded); assemble SourceMap.
  • set_source_content: grow sources_content to sources.len() with None, then assign index. Content embedding is opt-in, never default (per §6.10, ADR-017).

5.3 Deliverable

smap-builder crate: construct → add_source/add_name/set_source_content/add_mapping (any order) → into_sourcemap() yields a SourceMap whose mappings decodes (via smap-codec) back to exactly the inserted segments (modulo dedup + sort). cargo test -p smap-builder green.

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

# Criterion Measure
A1 Interning stable same string → same u32; distinct strings → distinct, insertion-ordered
A2 Order independence shuffled add_mapping ⇒ byte-identical mappings
A3 Round-trip decode_mappings(into_sourcemap().mappings) == inserted set (post sort/dedup)
A4 Dedup N identical add_mapping ⇒ 1 segment
A5 Content alignment sources_content[i] ↔ sources[i]; gaps None
A6 Version output version == 3; never panics

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input → Expected
builder/basic.json 3 sources, 5 mappings out-of-order → canonical sorted mappings
builder/intern_dedup.json repeated add_source("a.js") → single index 0
builder/gen_only.json add_mapping(gen, None, None) → 1-field segment encodes as single VLQ
builder/sparse_content.json set_source_content(2,..) w/ 0,1 unset → sourcesContent:[null,null,"…"]
builder/jridgewell_genmapping.json port @jridgewell/gen-mapping golden → identical output
Malformed: add_mapping w/ source ≥ sources.len() debug_assert/Result::Err at into_sourcemap, not silent OOB

5.6 Pitfalls

  • Unstable sort drops last-writer-wins: two segments same gen_column reorder nondeterministically → flaky golden. Root cause: sort_unstable. Fix: stable secondary key = insertion order; debug by diffing two runs.
  • Dedup before sort: equal segments non-adjacent pre-sort → dedup misses them. Order: sort then dedup.
  • sources_content desync: pushing content via separate Vec w/o index alignment → wrong source's content. Fix: index-keyed set_source_content + None backfill.
  • gen-only segment leaks source delta: encoding a 1-field segment but still advancing prev_source corrupts later 4-field deltas. Builder must mark gen-only segments so codec emits exactly 1 field (see Phase 3 reset rules).
  • Interning empty/None source: sources permits Option<String> (skipped sources) — interning "" ≠ a None slot; keep distinct.
  • 1-based input at API boundary: callers may pass 1-based lines; builder stores 0-based — convert at the call site, never inside (matches codec convention).

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • Target: 1M add_mapping in < 80 ms; into_sourcemap (sort+encode) for 1M segs < 150 ms.
  • Interner HashMap amortized O(1); pre-reserve sources/names if caller hints count.
  • Per-line Vec<Segment> grows lazily; add_mapping is push-only (no sort) → amortized O(1). Sort cost O(L·k log k) deferred to into_sourcemap.
  • Segment = 5×u32+2 Option tags ≈ 24 B; 1M segs ≈ 24 MB. Avoid per-segment heap.

5.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty builder → mappings: "", sources: [], valid v3.
  • Sparse generated lines (gaps) → empty Vecs encode as consecutive ;.
  • Duplicate gen_column different orig → both kept (not dups), sorted stably.
  • add_name w/o any add_mapping referencing it → name retained in names (unused-name policy: keep).
  • Very large gen.line (e.g. 10^7) → lazy extend bounded; reject u32::MAX overflow.

5.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
source index OOB into_sourcemap bounds check Err/debug_assert, name the bad index
non-monotonic post-sort validate.rs invariant panic in debug, log + skip in release? No — return error
encode error from codec encode_mappings is infallible; precondition violated upstream builder guarantees valid DecodedMap
content/source length mismatch into_sourcemap pads to sources.len() always aligned on emit

5.10 Operational Notes

  • Builder is single-threaded, owns its buffers; no interior mutability. For parallel codegen, build N builders then merge via smap-compose/smap-index, not shared state.
  • into_sourcemap consumes self (move) → no post-emit mutation; re-emit requires a fresh builder.
  • Expose add_source/add_name return indices so callers cache them (hot path: intern once, reuse u32).
  • NON-GOAL: no JS parsing, no AST — caller supplies (gen, orig) positions (that is #264).

18. Phase 6 — The consumer/query API (SourceMapConsumer)

6.1 Objectives

  • Build smap-consumer: SourceMapConsumer over a decoded map, answering position queries the way browsers/error reporters do.
  • new decodes "mappings" once, builds two sorted indexes: by-generated (gen_line, gen_col), by-original (source, orig_line, orig_col).
  • original_position_for(gen, Bias) → Option<OriginalLocation> via binary search; generated_position_for(source, orig, Bias) → Option<Position>; source_content_for(source); all_generated_positions_for.
  • Bias::GreatestLowerBound (default) / LeastUpperBound. Match reference source-map / trace-mapping query results on a shared corpus.
  • NON-GOAL: editing/composing maps (Phases 7/9); decode internals (Phase 3, reused via decode_mappings).

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File Adds
smap-consumer/src/lib.rs re-exports SourceMapConsumer, Bias; pub use smap_model::OriginalLocation
smap-consumer/src/consumer.rs SourceMapConsumer{ map:SourceMap, decoded:DecodedMap, by_gen:Vec<GenEntry>, by_orig:Vec<OrigEntry> }; new; query methods
smap-consumer/src/index.rs GenEntry{gen_line,gen_col,seg_ref}, OrigEntry{source,orig_line,orig_col,gen_line,gen_col,name}; index builders + comparators
smap-consumer/src/bias.rs Bias; search_glb/search_lub over a sorted slice + key extractor
  • new(map): decoded = decode_mappings(&map.mappings)? (CodecError propagated). Flatten DecodedMap → by_gen keeping (line, col) order (already sorted by codec invariant; assert in debug). Build by_orig from segments with source, then sort by (source, orig_line, orig_col, gen_line, gen_col) (per INV-7, ADR-011).
  • original_position_for: search_glb/search_lub on by_gen keyed by (gen_line, gen_col); require same gen_line for GLB hit (line-bounded, per MM4); map → OriginalLocation{source, line, col, name}. 1-field segments (no source) → skip / not a candidate. Bias is explicit, never implicit (per §6.8, ADR-012).
  • generated_position_for: search_glb/search_lub on by_orig keyed by (source, orig_line, orig_col); return earliest matching Position{gen_line,gen_col}. This is a GLB/LUB on the by-original sorted array (per MM4).
  • all_generated_positions_for(source, orig_line): lower/upper-bound the (source, orig_line) run in by_orig, return all Positions on that original line.
  • source_content_for(source): map.sources_content.get(source).and_then(Option::as_deref). Zero-copy borrow — returns &str lifetime tied to self (per §6.9, ADR-015).
  • Indexes built eagerly in new (no interior mutability / lazy cell) → &self queries, Send + Sync (per §11.7).

6.3 Deliverable

smap-consumer crate: SourceMapConsumer::new + four query methods compiling no_std+alloc; smap query original-for <line> <col> / generated-for CLI paths resolve through it; query-correctness + bias test matrix green.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • new decodes once: ≤1 decode_mappings call; query methods allocate zero Vec (except all_generated_positions_for).
  • original_position_for / generated_position_for match reference source-map output on the shared corpus: byte-identical (source,line,col,name) for 100% of probe points (both biases).
  • GLB query just past last segment on a line returns that segment; LUB returns next; cross-line GLB does not leak onto prior line.
  • Binary search: O(log n) per query, verified by op-count assertion on a 1M-segment map (≤21 comparisons).

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
consumer/basic.map + probes.json each (genL,genC,bias) → exact OriginalLocation
consumer/bias_glb.json / bias_lub.json same probe, GLB vs LUB diverge at segment boundaries
consumer/one_field.map 1-field segments → original_position_for = None
consumer/reverse.json generated_position_for round-trips every original→generated
consumer/all_gen.json all_generated_positions_for(src,line) → full position list (multi-hit)
Malformed: bad_mappings.map, source_oob.map new → Err(CodecError::{Vlq,SourceIndexOob}), no panic

6.6 Pitfalls

  • GLB leaking across lines → root cause: searching flat by_gen without line guard; a query column past EOL on line k matches line k+1's first segment. Fix: bound match to same gen_line. Debug: probe (k, BIG_COL).
  • Off-by-one between biases → GLB returns <=, LUB returns >=; reusing one partition_point for both inverts the boundary case (exact match). Decide: exact match belongs to GLB. Debug: probe a column exactly on a segment.
  • by_orig multi-key sort instability → many gen positions share one original; without (gen_line,gen_col) tiebreak generated_position_for returns a nondeterministic frame. Fix: full 5-tuple sort key.
  • Forgetting 1-field segments have no source → indexing them into by_orig panics on source.unwrap(). Fix: filter Some(source) when building by_orig.
  • 0-based vs 1-based at boundary → consumer is 0-based internally; CLI passes 1-based. Convert only in smap-cli, never in consumer.rs. Debug: off-by-one in CLI output but green unit tests.

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • new: one decode + two sorts. by_gen typically presorted (codec invariant) → sort_unstable_by near-linear; by_orig is the cost, O(n log n).
  • Query: binary search, no allocation, branch-predictable. Target <150 ns/query on a warm 1M-segment map.
  • Index memory: by_gen ~16 B/seg, by_orig ~24 B/seg → ~40 B/seg overhead atop DecodedMap. Ceiling stated in Phase 12.
  • Consider storing seg_ref as (line:u32, idx:u32) not a pointer → keeps indexes Copy, cache-dense, relocatable.

6.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty "mappings" → both indexes empty → all queries None.
  • Query before first segment (col 0, no seg at 0) → GLB None, LUB first segment.
  • Segment with name=None → OriginalLocation.name = None.
  • Duplicate gen positions (dedup not applied upstream) → GLB returns the last, LUB the first of the run; document tiebreak.
  • source index valid in segment but sources_content[source] = None → source_content_for = None (mapping still valid).
  • generated_position_for for a source/line with no mapping → None.

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
new on corrupt mappings decode_mappings Err propagate CodecError; no partial consumer
source index ≥ sources.len() check in new Err(CodecError::SourceIndexOob)
Query with out-of-range source arg bounds check return None (not panic)
Index/decoded desync (internal bug) debug_assert sorted + in-range fail loud in debug; never in release path

6.10 Operational Notes

  • SourceMapConsumer is immutable post-new → safe to Arc share across threads in an error-reporting service.
  • Construction is the expensive step (decode+sort); build once per map, reuse across millions of stack-frame queries.
  • Differential harness: feed identical maps+probes to smap and the JS source-map/trace-mapping, diff (source,line,col,name); any mismatch is a release blocker.
  • CLI smap query reports 1-based lines/cols and the resolved source name; --bias glb|lub selects bias (default glb).

19. Phase 7 — Composition / chaining (the correctness-critical crate)

Implements smap-compose. Folds a build pipeline's per-stage maps into ONE map so a position in the final artifact resolves directly to the original. Correctness-critical: every other crate is a codec; this crate is a semantic join, the #1 source of "off-by-one in minified stack trace" bugs.

7.1 Objectives

  • compose(orig_to_mid, mid_to_gen, mid_names) -> SourceMap: for each gen segment, resolve its mid position through orig_to_mid, emit the DEEPER original (source/line/col + name).
  • remap(chain: &[SourceMap]) -> Result<SourceMap, CodecError>: left-fold compose over an ordered chain (TS→JS→bundle→minify), index 0 = deepest/original.
  • Invariants: associativity ((a∘b)∘c == a∘(b∘c)); no-loss-of-resolvable-mappings (any gen pos resolvable end-to-end stays resolvable); null-segment propagation; sources/sourcesContent/names merged + de-duplicated.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File (crates/smap-compose/src/) Contents
lib.rs re-exports compose, remap; depends smap-consumer + smap-builder + smap-model
compose.rs compose() core join loop; MergeTable (mid-source→new-source remap)
remap.rs remap() fold; empty-chain + single-element fast paths
merge.rs SourceMerger (interns sources+content into a new builder, returns index map Vec<u32>)
  • compose algorithm (per gen segment S in mid_to_gen.0[line][i], with abs gen (line, S.gen_column)):
    1. S.source.is_none() → push 1-field (gen_column) only. (already gen-only; null-segment pass-through per §6.6, INV-8)
    2. lookup orig_to_mid.original_position_for(Position{S.orig_line,S.orig_column}, Bias::GLB) (GLB is the canonical lookup bias per §6.5, ADR-012; MM4):
      • Some(loc) → builder.add_mapping(gen, Some((merger.map(loc.source), Position{loc.line,loc.column})), deeper_name)
      • None → add_mapping(gen, None, None) (null/1-field; mid was a real mapping but its origin is unknown — propagate as gen-only per §6.6, MM3, never drop or snap to nearest).
  • deeper_name: prefer loc.name (origin side) → else S.name (mid side) re-interned. Name follows deepest available mapping (per §6.13, INV-8).
  • SourceMerger: copies orig_to_mid's sources + sourcesContent + sourceRoot into builder; mid_to_gen's file/sourceRoot win for output envelope. Identical (sourceRoot+path) pairs collapse to one index. Composition is the fold of partial functions (per MM3, MM7).
  • remap: acc = chain[0].clone(); for next in &chain[1..] { acc = compose(&Consumer::new(acc)?, &decode_mappings(&next.mappings)?, &next.names) }. Errors bubble as CodecError. remap is associative (per §6.5, INV-8) so fold order does not affect the semantic result.

7.3 Deliverable

smap-compose crate: compose + remap, an end-to-end 4-stage chain test (TS→JS→bundle→minify) where a minified (line,col) traces to the original .ts (line,col,name), and a differential harness vs Mozilla source-map's SourceMapConsumer.applySourceMap. cargo test -p smap-compose green.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 4-stage remap of fixtures yields a map where ≥100 sampled gen positions match the directly-composed reference within exact (source,line,col); mismatch rate 0.
  • Differential vs applySourceMap: ≥1000 random (gen→orig) queries on composed map equal across both impls (modulo source-path normalization), 0 disagreements.
  • Associativity test: remap([a,b,c]) byte-identical (after canonical re-encode) to compose(compose(a,b),c) and compose(a,compose(b,c)).
  • No-loss: count of resolvable gen positions in composed map ≥ count resolvable through the full chain sequentially.

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

File (tests/fixtures/compose/) Expected
ts_to_js.map, js_to_bundle.map, bundle_to_min.map inputs; chain order deepest-first
chain_flattened.expected.json remap output; canonical to_json byte-match
trace_samples.csv rows gen_line,gen_col → src,orig_line,orig_col,name; asserted post-remap
unmapped_mid.map mid segment with no origin → composed emits 1-field gen-only
Malformed: chain_version_mismatch/ (version≠3 → JsonError::BadVersion), empty_chain (remap([]) → Err(CodecError) or documented empty map), cyclic_self (map naming its own output → must terminate, no infinite fold)

7.6 Pitfalls

  • Composing the wrong direction: compose(orig_to_mid, mid_to_gen) — args are deepest-first. Swapping yields plausible-but-wrong traces. Debug: assert orig_to_mid.file == mid_to_gen.sources[*] linkage at entry.
  • Querying mid with GLB silently borrows a neighbor: a mid pos between two real mappings resolves to the prior segment; correct per spec but produces "drifted" origins if mid map is sparse. Debug: log GLB delta; flag deltas > line threshold.
  • Dropping unmapped-mid segments instead of emitting gen-only: removes a generated column that DID exist → downstream binary search returns the wrong neighbor. Root cause: treating None lookup as "skip". Always emit 1-field.
  • Name index re-interning forgotten: copying loc.name/S.name indices verbatim into the new map points into the WRONG names array. Debug: every name must route through builder.add_name.
  • sourcesContent lost across hops: only mid_to_gen's content survives unless merger pulls origin content. Verify sourcesContent[i].is_some() count is preserved.
  • sourceRoot double-applied: joining root+path at both hops doubles the prefix. Normalize to absolute once in SourceMerger.

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • compose is O(Σ segments in mid_to_gen × log(orig segments)) — each gen segment = one original_position_for binary search. Build orig_to_mid consumer index ONCE, reuse across all segments.
  • remap over N-stage chain rebuilds a SourceMapConsumer per fold step (decode + sort): O(stages × map size). Acceptable for stages ≤ 8; target < 50 ms for a 4-stage chain of 100 K-segment maps.
  • SourceMerger source de-dup via HashMap<(root,path), u32>; avoid O(sources²) linear scan.
  • Pre-size the output builder's per-line buffers from mid_to_gen line count.

7.8 Edge Cases

  • Mid map has MORE generated lines than orig has mappings → trailing gen-only segments.
  • orig_to_mid empty (identity-ish) → composed == mid_to_gen re-sourced.
  • Single-stage remap([m]) → returns m re-encoded (no compose), preserving names/content.
  • Segment on mid line beyond mid_to_gen.0.len() → impossible by construction; assert.
  • Duplicate gen columns post-compose → builder dedup keeps first (deepest origin wins by sort stability).
  • Name present on mid but origin segment nameless → keep mid name (deeper-available rule).

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Chain stage fails to decode decode_mappings → CodecError::Vlq/BadFieldCount abort remap, return error with stage index
Version mismatch in chain Consumer::new rejects non-3 surface JsonError::BadVersion at fold
Self-referential / cyclic chain fold is bounded by chain.len() (no graph walk) terminates; no cycle possible in a &[SourceMap]
OOB source index after merge merger.map panics on unknown → convert to CodecError::SourceIndexOob reject map

7.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: smap compose a.map b.map ... > out.map (args deepest-first, documented in --help); smap flatten is the smap-index sibling, not this.
  • Telemetry: emit composed_segments, gen_only_emitted, unresolved_mid counts to stderr under --verbose for debugging drift.
  • Determinism: compose MUST be deterministic — fixed merge order, stable sort — so CI byte-match on chain_flattened.expected.json holds.
  • NON-GOAL: composing does not minify or re-parse code; it only joins maps. Bundler/transpiler integration (Phase 10) calls remap.

20. Phase 8 — Index (sectioned) maps

Implements smap-index: the sectioned-map model + flatten(). Scope: build IndexedSourceMap from (offset, SourceMap) chunks (concatenated bundle sections); enforce sorted + non-overlapping invariants; flatten to a flat SourceMap losslessly. Non-goals: composition (Phase 7 smap-compose), JSON I/O (Phase 4 emits the "sections"/"offset" envelope), editing (Phase 9).

8.1 Objectives

  • Model IndexedSourceMap{ version:3, file, sections:[Section{offset:Position, map:SourceMap}] } per v3 sectioned spec; Section.map is a regular (non-index) v3 map.
  • Construct from an ordered list of (offset, SourceMap) chunks; one chunk = one emitted output region (e.g. concatenated module).
  • Invariants: sections sorted ascending by offset (line, then column); non-overlapping; no nested index map in a section.
  • flatten() -> SourceMap: shift each section's generated positions by its offset, merge sources/sourcesContent/names with index remapping, re-encode mappings.
  • Lossless: flatten output ≡ a flat map built directly from the same inputs (segment sets equal after sort/dedup).

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • smap-index/src/lib.rs: pub struct Section{ pub offset: Position, pub map: SourceMap }, pub struct IndexedSourceMap{ pub version: u8, pub file: Option<String>, pub sections: Vec<Section> }.
  • IndexedSourceMap::from_sections(file, Vec<(Position, SourceMap)>) -> Result<Self, IndexError> — validates sort/overlap, sets version=3 (per INV-9).
  • validate(&self) -> Result<(), IndexError>: sections strictly increasing by (offset.line, offset.column); overlap check uses each section's decoded extent (max generated line/col, see 8.8). Sorted + non-overlapping = the INV-9 invariant.
  • flatten(&self) -> Result<SourceMap, CodecError>: decode each map.mappings (smap-codec::decode_mappings), shift, merge via a SourceMapBuilder (Phase 5), then into_sourcemap.
  • Offset arithmetic (per section, per segment): gen_line' = seg_line + offset.line; gen_col' = if seg_line==0 { seg_col + offset.column } else { seg_col } — column offset applies ONLY to the section's first generated line (per §6.14, INV-9, MM6, ADR-014). This is the #1 pitfall in this phase.
  • Index remap on merge: build per-section src_remap: Vec<u32> / name_remap: Vec<u32> via builder interners (add_source/add_name); rewrite each Segment.source/Segment.name through the remap before add_mapping (per INV-5, INV-13).
  • pub enum IndexError { Unsorted{at:usize}, Overlap{prev:usize,next:usize}, NestedIndexMap{at:usize}, VersionMismatch(u8) }; flatten surfaces CodecError from decode/encode.

8.3 Deliverable

smap-index crate: IndexedSourceMap/Section types, from_sections, validate, flatten. cargo test -p smap-index green: build an index map from ≥3 chunks and assert flatten() equals a directly-built flat map. CLI smap flatten <index.json> (wired Phase 0 stub → real here) prints the flat map.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Round-trip: for N chunks, flatten(index).decode segment-set == flat-builder segment-set (order-insensitive, post-dedup) — byte-identical mappings after canonical sort.
  • validate rejects unsorted (Unsorted), overlapping (Overlap), and nested (NestedIndexMap) inputs; accepts adjacent (touching, non-overlapping) sections.
  • First-line column offset applied exactly once per section; lines 2..n of a section keep original gen_col.
  • sources/names merged with no duplicate entries; every flattened segment's indices resolve in the merged tables.
  • Empty section (no mappings) contributes nothing; never panics.

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
index_two_section.json 2 sections, offsets (0,0),(10,0) flatten == index_two_flat.json (golden)
index_col_offset.json offset (5,7), multi-line section line-0 cols +7; line-1+ unchanged
index_shared_sources.json both sections cite a.js merged sources len==1, indices remapped
index_adjacent.json sections touch exactly accepted; no Overlap
bad_unsorted.json offsets (10,0) then (0,0) IndexError::Unsorted
bad_overlap.json section 1 extent crosses section 2 offset IndexError::Overlap
bad_nested.json section map is itself an index map IndexError::NestedIndexMap

8.6 Pitfalls

  • Column offset applied to ALL lines of a section → wrong cols on lines 2+. Root cause: forgetting seg_line==0 guard. Debug: diff flat vs golden, first divergent segment on line 2 of a section.
  • Section extent computed from offset alone (assuming next offset bounds it) → false Overlap/missed overlap. Root cause: a section's content can exceed/underrun the gap to the next offset. Debug: decode + compute true max generated position.
  • Forgetting to remap source/name indices on merge → segments point into the wrong source after concat. Debug: query a flattened position, wrong filename returned.
  • Sort-by-offset alone doesn't dedup cross-section segments at identical generated positions → duplicate after shift; rely on builder dedup post-shift.
  • sourceRoot differing per section silently dropped on flatten → resolve to absolute/normalized sources (defer policy to Phase 10) or fail loud; do NOT pick one section's root arbitrarily.

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • flatten is O(total_segments): decode each section once, single merge pass, one encode. No per-segment binary search (unlike compose).
  • Pre-size builder buffers from Σ section segment counts (cheap upper bound = Σ mappings.len()/2) to avoid Vec regrowth.
  • Source/name merge via HashMap interner: O(1) amortized per distinct entry; reuse builder's interner, don't allocate a new map per section.
  • Large bundles (10⁴ sections): keep sections borrowed; flatten streams section-by-section, peak mem ≈ one decoded section + growing flat output.

8.8 Edge Cases

  • Single section at (0,0): flatten == that section's map verbatim (modulo index reindex).
  • Empty sections: flatten → empty flat map (mappings:"", empty tables), version:3.
  • Section with empty mappings but non-empty sources: sources still merged (sourcesContent retained); no segments shifted.
  • Multi-line section: extent = (max gen_line over its segments)+offset.line; non-overlap checked against this, not the section's last offset.
  • Section whose offset column > 0 but first generated line is empty: column offset is a no-op (no line-0 segments).
  • Offsets with equal line, increasing column: valid; column offset disambiguates same-line sections.

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Overlapping sections validate extent check IndexError::Overlap{prev,next}; reject before flatten
Nested index map in section shape check on Section.map IndexError::NestedIndexMap{at}
Section map decode error decode_mappings → CodecError propagate from flatten, name offending section index
Index/name OOB after merge builder validation (Phase 5) CodecError::SourceIndexOob
version != 3 from_sections/validate IndexError::VersionMismatch

8.10 Operational Notes

  • smap inspect <index.json> reports section count, per-section offset + extent + segment count; smap flatten emits the flat map (--inline for data: URL).
  • Bundlers (#264) emit one section per concatenated module pre-shift; smap-index is the cheaper alternative to full compose when chunks are independently mapped — use index maps for concat, compose for transform chains.
  • Browsers/devtools consume "sections" natively; flatten is for tools/error-reporters that only accept flat maps. Document both paths.
  • Validate-before-ship: CI runs smap validate on every emitted index map; non-overlap + sort are hard gates.

21. Phase 9 — MagicString-style source editor

9.1 Objectives

  • Build smap-edit: a MagicString editor over an original UTF-8 buffer; apply ordered edits (overwrite/append/prepend/remove/move/indent) WITHOUT re-parsing.
  • Maintain a chunk/interval list mapping each output range -> its original byte range (or marking it synthetic/introduced).
  • to_string() emits transformed code by concatenating live chunks in output order; generate_map(opts) emits a SourceMap (via SourceMapBuilder) tracing every unmodified + moved char back to its true original (line,col).
  • NON-GOAL: parsing/tokenizing JS, AST edits, minification — smap-edit is byte-range surgery only; positions come from the caller.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

  • Files: smap-edit/src/lib.rs, chunk.rs (chunk model + split), magic.rs (MagicString ops), emit.rs (to_string + generate_map), lines.rs (byte-offset -> Position index over original).
  • Data structures:
    • struct Chunk { orig_start: usize, orig_end: usize, content: String, intro: String, outro: String, edited: bool, kind: ChunkKind }; enum ChunkKind { Original, Introduced }.
    • Doubly-linked chunk order via Vec<Chunk> + next/prev: Option<usize> indices (move = relink, not memmove); by_start: BTreeMap<usize,usize> for O(log n) split lookup.
    • struct LineIndex { line_starts: Vec<usize> } over original buffer; offset_to_pos(byte)->Position via binary search; column = chars (not bytes) from line start.
  • Algorithms:
    • split_at(byte): find chunk containing byte, split into two Original chunks at the byte boundary (idempotent if already a boundary); precondition for every range op. Byte offsets are into the original UTF-8 buffer; column tracking converts to UTF-16 code units at the boundary (per §6.4, MM5, §28.5, F6).
    • overwrite(s,e,c): split_at(s), split_at(e); mark inner chunk(s) edited, set content=c, clear orig link for map (output range -> NO original unless opts.keep_names/identity) (per ADR-018).
    • remove(s,e): split; set inner content="", edited; chunk stays in order as zero-width (carries no segment — null-segment propagation per §6.6).
    • append/prepend(str): push Introduced chunk at tail/head; Chunk.intro/outro for boundary insertions around an existing chunk.
    • move(s,e,index): split at s,e,index; relink the [s,e) chunk run before chunk at index; original ranges PRESERVED → moved chars still map to true origin (not output position — per INV-13, MM5).
    • indent(prefix): insert prefix after every \n boundary; each inserted prefix is Introduced (unmapped); following original text keeps its origin but at shifted output column.
  • to_string (emit.rs): walk linked order; emit intro + content + outro; for Original unedited chunks content is the original slice.
  • generate_map: walk output in order tracking (out_line,out_col) in UTF-16 code units (per §28.5, F6); for each LIVE original chunk, add_mapping at every output line-start AND at the chunk's first char; map (out_line,out_col) -> offset_to_pos(orig_start); advance both cursors counting \n to keep columns correct; Introduced/edited chunks emit NO segment (or a 1-field gen-only marker if opts.hires=false) (per §6.6, ADR-018).
  • MapOptions { file, source, source_root, include_content: bool, hires: bool }; include_content -> set_source_content(0, original).

9.3 Deliverable

  • smap-edit crate: MagicString::new(orig), ops returning &mut Self (chainable), to_string(), generate_map(&MapOptions)->SourceMap.
  • A doctest-style example chaining overwrite + move + prepend, emitting code + map; map feeds smap-consumer and traces a moved identifier to its original line/col.

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Property: every unmodified char c at output pos P, consumer.original_position_for(P,GLB) == true origin 100% over fuzzed edit sequences
Moved chunk: chars trace to ORIGINAL location, not output exact (line,col)
to_string() byte-correct vs hand-applied edits byte-identical
Multi-line overwrite/indent column tracking exact, 0 drift
generate_map over 100k-char buffer + 1k edits < 5 ms
Never panics on overlapping/zero-width/out-of-range ops returns or no-ops

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input ops Expected
edit/overwrite_single.json overwrite ident foo->bar code swapped; segment for bar is gen-only/unmapped; trailing text re-anchored
edit/move_block.json move stmt to head moved chars map to original lines; to_string reordered
edit/indent_block.json indent(" ") over 3 lines each line +2 cols; origins shifted; prefixes unmapped
edit/multiline_insert.json prepend "a\nb\n" following code shifts +2 out_lines; origins intact
edit/remove_range.json remove [5,9) gap closed; surrounding chars map correctly
Malformed: overwrite(10,4,..) (start>end) — Err/no-op, no panic
Malformed: move into own range — rejected, no-op
Malformed: byte offset splitting a UTF-8 boundary — snapped to char boundary or Err

9.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
Moved chars map to output position added segment used out-cursor for origin assert origin = offset_to_pos(orig_start), not out pos
Column drift after multi-line insert counted bytes not \n/chars when advancing unit test inserts with embedded \n; diff expected col
Off-by-one after split_at at existing boundary double-split creates empty chunk poisoning order make split_at idempotent; assert no zero-orig-width Original chunks
Overwrite leaks original mapping edited chunk still carries orig_start link set edited -> suppress segment unless identity-preserving
UTF-8 panic on &orig[s..e] byte offset mid-codepoint validate is_char_boundary; snap or Err
indent double-prefixes blank lines applied prefix to \n\n second newline option indent_empty=false; test consecutive newlines

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • Chunk ops O(log n) split via BTreeMap; move = O(1) relink; avoid Vec::insert/memmove of chunk array.
  • to_string: single pass, pre-size String to orig.len() + intro/outro deltas; no per-char realloc.
  • generate_map: emit segments only at chunk starts + line boundaries (NOT per char) unless hires; reuse SourceMapBuilder interners (single source).
  • LineIndex built once O(n); offset_to_pos O(log lines) binary search.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty buffer; single-char buffer; edit covering entire buffer.
  • Zero-width op (remove(s,s), overwrite(s,s,..)) -> pure insertion.
  • Adjacent edits sharing a boundary; nested/overlapping overwrites (last-write or Err).
  • Move to start (index 0) / to end (index==len); move adjacent to source (no-op).
  • Content with \r\n vs \n; trailing newline / no trailing newline.
  • append/prepend on empty original; generate_map with include_content=false.

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Out-of-range / start>end bounds check in split_at return &mut Self no-op (or Result in strict mode)
UTF-8 mid-codepoint split is_char_boundary guard snap to boundary or Err
Overlapping overwrite interval intersection check vs edited chunks reject second op
Map drift (silent) property test vs buffer in CI fail build; bisect failing op

9.10 Operational Notes

  • MapOptions.hires: true = per-char-segment fidelity (bigger map); false = boundary-only (smaller) — document size/precision tradeoff.
  • Default include_content=true so emitted maps are self-contained for smap-consumer/devtools.
  • smap-cli: no direct subcommand; smap-edit is a library consumed by #264 (Phase 10) and tests; expose via integration test only.
  • Surface to_string() + generate_map() are pure (no I/O); callers own file writes + //# sourceMappingURL= emission (Phase 4).

22. Phase 10 — Bundler / transpiler integration

The integration surface #264 (Bundler/Transpiler) and any transpiler/minifier wire codegen into. NON-GOAL: this phase parses/transforms no JS — #264 owns AST/print/minify; we own the map produced alongside. Consumes Phases 5 (smap-builder), 7 (smap-compose), 8 (smap-index), 9 (smap-edit).

10.1 Objectives

  • Define EmitMapper handle the codegen Emitter calls per emitted token: add_token(gen, Option<(source, orig)>, name) → thin wrapper over SourceMapBuilder::add_mapping.
  • Compose per-stage maps (lower → print → minify) into one output map via smap-compose::remap; concatenated-output maps via smap-index (sectioned) or a single flattened SourceMapBuilder.
  • Codify policy knobs: sourcesContent embedding (opt-in, dedup), sourceRoot + relative-path normalization (paths relative to map location), ignoreList (vendored/runtime) so debuggers skip frames.
  • Produce a correct composed map for the #264 worked-example bundle; documented, tested integration surface.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

New crate? No — integration lives in smap-builder (builder/integration.rs) to avoid a cycle (#264 depends on smap-builder + smap-compose); re-exports from smap facade.

// smap-builder::integration
pub struct EmitMapper { b: SourceMapBuilder, opts: IntegrationOpts }
pub struct IntegrationOpts {
    pub embed_sources_content: bool,   // default false (opt-in)
    pub source_root: Option<String>,   // emitted verbatim into envelope
    pub normalize_paths: bool,         // sources made relative to map_dir
    pub map_dir: Option<String>,       // dir holding the .map (for relativization)
    pub ignore_globs: Vec<String>,     // matched -> ignoreList indices
}
impl EmitMapper {
    pub fn new(opts: IntegrationOpts) -> Self;
    pub fn intern_source(&mut self, path: &str) -> u32;   // dedup via builder interner
    pub fn intern_name(&mut self, n: &str) -> u32;
    pub fn add_token(&mut self, gen: Position, orig: Option<(u32, Position)>, name: Option<u32>);
    pub fn set_content(&mut self, source: u32, content: &str);  // honored only if embed=true
    pub fn finish(self) -> SourceMap;  // normalize paths, mark ignoreList, into_sourcemap
}
pub fn flatten_builders(parts: Vec<(Position /*offset*/, SourceMap)>) -> SourceMap; // concat path
  • Per-token emit: Emitter calls add_token once per emitted run (not per char) — gen from print cursor, orig from AST node span. Mapper forwards to add_mapping; orig=None ⇒ 1-field (generated-only) segment (per §6.6, MM3). Columns are UTF-16 code units (per §28.5, F6); convert byte offsets from the printer at this boundary only (per MM5).
  • Staged compose: each stage returns its SourceMap; bundler calls remap(&[lower, print, minify]) (Phase 7) — fold compose orig→mid→gen (per MM7, INV-8); null-segment propagation on unmapped mid (per §6.6).
  • Concatenation: two routes — (a) smap-index sectioned map (one Section{offset, map} per input chunk, flatten() later) cheap to build, deferred offset math (per §6.14, ADR-014); (b) flatten_builders merges chunk maps into one SourceMapBuilder shifting gen positions by chunk offset (offset.line added to all lines; offset.column to first line only per MM6) — flat single map, higher build cost. #264 picks per output mode.
  • Path normalization (finish, if normalize_paths): each sources[i] → path relative to map_dir; forward-slash separators on all OS; leave None (anonymous) untouched. sourceRoot joined logically, NOT folded into sources (consumer reconstructs sourceRoot + source). Library never resolves paths to the filesystem — opaque labels only (per §25 T6, MM8).
  • sourcesContent: gated by embed_sources_content (per §6.10, ADR-017); sources_content[i] filled only for interned i; uninterned/duplicate paths share one slot (interner dedup) — never store content twice.
  • ignoreList: after interning, indices whose path matches any ignore_globs (e.g. **/node_modules/**, runtime shims) pushed to ignore_list (emitted as x_google_ignoreList), sorted/deduped (per §6.10).

10.3 Deliverable

smap-builder::integration (EmitMapper, IntegrationOpts, flatten_builders) + smap facade re-export. A worked-example test consuming #264's bundle fixture (3 ES modules → lowered → printed → minified → concatenated) emitting one composed SourceMap; cargo test -p smap-builder integration green. Doc page: "Wiring a codegen into smap" (emit loop + compose + concat recipe).

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Worked-example composed map: query each (gen) frame resolves to correct (source, orig_line, orig_col) in original ES module
embed_sources_content=false sourcesContent absent/all-null; map size minimal
embed_sources_content=true, dup path interned twice content stored once; sources.len()==sources_content.len()
normalize_paths w/ map_dir=dist/ every sources[i] relative, /-separated, no ..\\ on Windows
ignore_globs=["**/vendor/**"] matching source indices ∈ ignore_list, sorted, unique
Concat route (a) flatten == route (b) flatten_builders byte-identical mappings after re-encode

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
integ/worked_bundle.in.json 3-module #264 bundle spec (spans) worked_bundle.map.json — composed, every frame resolves
integ/concat_sections.expected.json 2 chunk maps + offsets sectioned map; flatten() == concat_flat.expected.json
integ/content_embed.expected.json embed=true, dup source one content slot, lengths equal
integ/sourceroot_relpath.expected.json map_dir=dist/, abs sources relative /-paths + sourceRoot preserved
integ/ignorelist.expected.json vendored + app sources x_google_ignoreList=[idx…] sorted
MALFORMED integ/offset_overlap.in.json sections overlap Err(CodecError) from flatten (Phase 8 invariant)
MALFORMED integ/token_unsorted.in.json tokens out of gen order builder sort fixes OR ordering-validation Err (Phase 5)

10.6 Pitfalls

  • Double-relativizing sourceRoot: folding sourceRoot into sources AND emitting sourceRoot ⇒ consumer doubles prefix → 404 sources. Root cause: two normalizers. Debug: assert exactly one of {fold, emit}; check raw envelope.
  • Stale gen cursor across stages: composing maps whose generated coords are from different printer passes (column counted in bytes vs UTF-16 units) ⇒ off-by-N at multibyte chars. Root cause: column-unit mismatch with #264. Debug: pin both to UTF-16 code-unit columns (browser convention); diff a non-ASCII fixture.
  • Concat offset.column on every line: applying section offset.column to all lines, not just the section's first line, shifts every subsequent line. Root cause: misread flatten rule. Debug: 2-line section fixture, inspect line-2 first segment.
  • Content dedup keyed by content not path: two distinct files with identical bytes collapsed → wrong source_content_for. Root cause: dedup on value. Fix: key dedup on interned source index only.
  • ignoreList indices stale after sort/dedup of sources: builder reorders/dedups sources in into_sourcemap; ignoreList built on pre-sort indices points at wrong sources. Root cause: ordering between interning and ignore-marking. Debug: compute ignoreList AFTER into_sourcemap index assignment.
  • Per-char add_token: calling add_token per character (not per run) → millions of redundant segments, bloated map. Root cause: emitter granularity. Debug: assert segment count ≈ token count.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • add_token must be O(1) amortized — interners are hash maps; no per-token sort. Single sort at into_sourcemap (Phase 5): O(N log N).
  • Concat route (a) smap-index defers all offset math → build O(Σ chunks); flatten paid once on demand. Route (b) eager → O(N) merge but no later flatten cost.
  • embed_sources_content=true adds Σ|content| bytes to output; gate behind opt-in; never duplicate (interner). Target: ≤1.05× of separate-files content size.
  • remap over k stages: k passes of compose; each O(N log N) via consumer binary search — dominates for deep pipelines, acceptable at k≤4 typical.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • Zero-token output (empty file) ⇒ mappings:"", sources:[], valid envelope.
  • Generated-only tokens (injected runtime prelude, no original) ⇒ 1-field segments; resolve to None.
  • map_dir == None with normalize_paths=true ⇒ no-op (cannot relativize) — emit warning, leave absolute.
  • Source path == map path's dir prefix ⇒ relative "./x.js", never "".
  • Anonymous source (None in sources) under normalization ⇒ skip, stays None.
  • ignoreList glob matches zero sources ⇒ empty ignore_list, field omitted from JSON.
  • Concat with single chunk + zero offset ⇒ flatten is identity.

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Section overlap on concat flatten invariant check (Phase 8) Err(CodecError); #264 fixes offsets
Unsorted tokens from buggy emitter into_sourcemap ordering validation sort-or-Err per builder policy
Content embed requested, file unreadable by #264 set_content never called for idx slot stays None; map still valid
Compose with mismatched stage (mid coords absent) unmapped mid in compose 1-field generated-only segment (no panic)
Path normalization on mixed-OS separators normalize forces / deterministic regardless of host OS

10.10 Operational Notes

  • EmitMapper is single-threaded per output file; for parallel chunk codegen, build N independent maps → combine via route (a)/(b) at join.
  • Default policy: embed_sources_content=false, normalize_paths=true, empty ignore_globs — safe minimal map; #264 opts into embedding/ignore explicitly.
  • smap-cli inspect (Phase 0/12) used in CI to dump a composed map's source list + ignoreList for review of #264 bundles.
  • Contract with #264: columns are UTF-16 code units, lines/cols 0-based at the API boundary (Phase 2 rule); #264 converts 1-based user lines only at its own edges.
  • Version skew: EmitMapper always emits version:3; index maps carry version:3 per section + top level.

23. Phase 11 — Validation, conformance, and fuzzing

11.1 Objectives

  • Prove this toolkit matches the Source Map v3 spec + the pinned reference corpora ("source-map", "@jridgewell/sourcemap-codec") via differential decode/re-encode/query.
  • Stand conformance/ corpus harness + fuzz/ (cargo-fuzz/libfuzzer) targets: VLQ, mappings, JSON, roundtrip — never panic on arbitrary bytes.
  • Property-test EVERY section-7 invariant (delta resets, 0-based, bias monotonicity, compose associativity, flatten offset).
  • Emit a differential-parity.md report vs pinned reference versions; gate CI on green buckets + zero fuzz crashes for the budget.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module / file Work
conformance/ (new top-level dir) corpus/ (vendored fixtures), vendor.lock (ref versions + sha256), manifest.toml (bucket→files→expectations)
xtask/src/conformance.rs corpus runner: load fixture → drive smap-json::from_json/smap-codec::decode_mappings/smap-consumer → compare to *.expected.json
xtask/src/diff_ref.rs shells the pinned JS refs (node refgen/{decode,query}.mjs) over corpus; diffs DecodedMap + N query points; writes parity report
refgen/*.mjs thin adapters around @jridgewell/sourcemap-codec decode + source-map SourceMapConsumer (pinned in refgen/package-lock.json)
fuzz/fuzz_targets/vlq.rs decode_vlq arbitrary bytes; on Ok re-encode_vlq → re-decode == value (round-trip per INV-1)
fuzz/fuzz_targets/mappings.rs decode_mappings(arbitrary str); on Ok re-encode_mappings→decode == idempotent (per INV-2)
fuzz/fuzz_targets/json.rs from_json(arbitrary); on Ok → to_json→from_json stable; never panic/OOM (per INV-10)
fuzz/fuzz_targets/roundtrip.rs structured arbitrary → SourceMapBuilder → encode → decode → query == built segments (per §6.1)
smap-*/tests/prop_*.rs proptest strategies: arb_segment, arb_decoded_map, arb_chain, arb_index
  • Differential normalization: both sides canonicalized before diff (segments sorted by (gen_line,gen_col); trailing empty groups dropped; names/sources order-independent compare via index remap). Reference parity enforced against the spec oracle (per §6.1, ADR-021).

11.3 Deliverable

cargo xtask conformance (all buckets green), cargo fuzz run <t> -- -max_total_time=… (0 crashes for CI budget), cargo test --workspace (props pass), and a committed target/reports/differential-parity.md. Wired into CI make conformance + nightly fuzz job.

11.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause → debug
False diff vs ref on equal maps refs keep trailing ;/empty groups; ours trims → normalize BOTH before diff; dump canonical forms side-by-side
Ref query off-by-one "source-map" presents 1-based lines; our codec 0-based → convert ONLY at adapter boundary; assert in diff_ref.rs

Bias mismatch at exact hit GLB and LUB MUST return same segment when query == a segment's gen position; property catches asymmetry
Non-UTF8 data: base64 panics decoded inline map not valid UTF-8 → from_json must return JsonError::Parse, not unwrap

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Fuzz throughput target ≥50k exec/s (vlq), ≥10k exec/s (mappings/json) — keep targets allocation-light, reuse String buffer across iterations where the harness allows.
  • Conformance suite ≤30s wall on the largest corpus; cache decoded refs (refgen writes .expected.json once, committed) to avoid Node spawn per-run in CI.
  • Property cases capped (PROPTEST_CASES); shrinking bounded to avoid CI timeout on deep arb_chain.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty mappings:"" → empty DecodedMap; both sides agree.
  • Single 1-field segment (generated-only, no source) round-trips and queries to None original.
  • Map with sources:[null] / missing sourcesContent entry → consumer source_content_for → None, no diff.
  • Duplicate generated positions → consumer picks per Bias deterministically; differential must match ref tie-break.
  • ignoreList round-trips through to_json/from_json without affecting decode parity.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Containment
Codec regression vs ref nightly differential parity diff non-empty block merge; attach offending fixture + minimized diff
New fuzz crash corpus artifact in fuzz/artifacts/ auto-file fixture into corpus/regressions/, add to conformance
Ref version drift vendor.lock sha256 mismatch fail fast; refuse to run until lock re-pinned + report regenerated
Property shrink to minimal counterexample proptest persisted .proptest-regressions committed as permanent regression seed

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Pin refs by exact version + sha256 in conformance/vendor.lock; differential-parity.md records versions + pass counts + date.
  • CI: PR runs make conformance + 60s/target fuzz smoke; nightly runs extended fuzz (≥15min/target) + full corpus + regenerates parity report as artifact.
  • Every fuzz artifact + proptest counterexample is minimized and demoted to a permanent corpus/regressions/ fixture (no silent fixes).
  • Out of scope: fuzzing JS semantics (#264), perf benchmarking (Phase 12), wasm/DWARF maps (PhaseDeferred).

24. Phase 12 — Performance and scale

12.1 Objectives

  • Encode/decode maps with millions of segments at section-4 targets; zero panics, bounded memory.
  • Replace match-based VLQ decode with table-driven byte-at-a-time loop in smap-vlq; pre-size all output buffers.
  • Zero-copy JSON: smap-json borrows sources/names/sourcesContent slices from input (no per-string String alloc).
  • Lazy/streaming decode: query a single generated line's segments on demand without materializing full DecodedMap.
  • Establish memory ceilings, criterion micro/macro benches, and a regression gate (CI fails on >X% slowdown). Performance "done" gate.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Area Change Module / File
VLQ decode 256-entry const REV: [i8; 256] (-1 = invalid); decode_vlq loops bytes, shifts, checks bit-32 continuation (per ADR-005, §31.4) smap-vlq/src/lib.rs
VLQ encode write into caller &mut String via push on B64 table; no temp Vec (per §6.9) smap-vlq/src/lib.rs
Codec buffers encode_mappings reserves ~ segments * 7 bytes (per C9); decode reserves Vec::with_capacity(line_count) smap-codec/src/encode.rs, decode.rs
Zero-copy JSON SourceMapBorrowed<'a>{ sources:Vec<Option<&'a str>>, … } parsed from &'a str; into_owned() -> SourceMap (per §6.9, C6, ADR-015) smap-json/src/borrow.rs
Lazy decode LineCursor{ bytes:&str, line_offsets:Vec<u32> }; decode_line(n) runs DecoderState only for line n; per-line carry checkpoints store DecoderState at each ; to enable O(1) line seek (per §32 streaming section, MM2) smap-codec/src/lazy.rs
Streaming decode_mappings_into(&str, &mut impl FnMut(u32,&[Segment])) — per-line callback, no full Vec<Vec<_>> (per §37 Alt-2, §6.9) smap-codec/src/decode.rs
Benches benches/vlq.rs, benches/codec.rs, benches/consumer.rs, benches/index.rs (criterion per ADR-022) each crate benches/
  • line_offsets (byte index of each ;) built in one O(n) pass; enables O(1) jump to any line for lazy/consumer paths. Without per-line carry checkpoints, a jump to line i would need to replay all preceding segments — O(n) instead of O(segments-on-line-i) (per MM2, §32 carry-checkpoint).
  • Branch-reduction (optional): batch-classify 8 bytes via table lookups; SIMD memchr for ;/, scan, feature-gated simd (per §31.9, ADR-019).

12.3 Deliverable

  • Optimized smap-vlq/smap-codec/smap-json + LineCursor lazy path + per-line streaming API.
  • Criterion suites (micro + macro on real multi-MB maps) committed; make bench runs them; target/criterion baselines checked in.
  • A PERF.md table: measured VLQ ints/s, mappings MB/s, query µs, index-build ms, peak RSS — all meeting section-4 targets.

Metric Target Gate

mappings decode ≥ 200 MB/s criterion
mappings encode ≥ 150 MB/s criterion
original_position_for ≤ 1 µs warm (10M-seg map) criterion

Regression < 5% slowdown vs baseline CI fails
cargo build --no-default-features smap-vlq/-codec compile no_std CI

12.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
Quadratic String growth in encode no reserve; repeated realloc bench shows >2× regression on size doubling; add with_capacity
Zero-copy UB / dangling borrow SourceMapBorrowed outlives &'a str buffer lifetimes enforce; miri on borrow tests
Lazy line_offsets off-by-one trailing ; vs empty last line assert offsets.len()==lines; compare vs eager decoder
REV table misses +// partial alphabet init unit-test all 64 chars + 192 invalids = -1
SIMD path drifts from scalar feature-gated divergence differential test simd vs scalar on corpus in CI matrix
usize byte vs u32 column mismatch on >4 GB input truncation reject inputs > u32::MAX columns with explicit error

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Hot loop = decode_vlq: keep it #[inline], branch-light (table lookup + shift), no bounds-check via get_unchecked only after length precheck.
  • Allocation dominates large maps: pre-size mappings String and per-line Vec<Segment>; reuse EncoderState/DecoderState across lines.
  • Zero-copy avoids N source-string copies (sourcesContent can be MBs each); borrow until into_owned.
  • Consumer: sort once (sort_unstable_by_key), cache by-gen + by-orig indexes; queries are pure binary search, O(log n).

12.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty mappings / all-; map → zero segments, instant; no underflow in offset math.
  • One mega-line, no ; (5M segments) → must not be O(n²); single Vec::reserve up front.
  • Map larger than RAM → streaming/per-line callback path; never hold full DecodedMap.
  • Many duplicate sources/names → interner + zero-copy keeps memory flat.
  • Pathological VLQ (max-width i64 groups) → decode stays within i64, Overflow else; no panic.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Mitigation
OOM on multi-GB map RSS ceiling assert in macro-bench route to streaming API; document limit
Silent perf regression criterion baseline diff in CI gate at 5%; block merge
Bench noise / flaky gate CV across 100 samples warm-up + min 100 samples; compare medians not means
Borrow panic in release miri + debug asserts lifetime-checked API; fuzz borrow path

12.10 Operational Notes

  • make bench → criterion; make bench-baseline saves; CI compares PR vs main baseline, fails > 5%.
  • Macro-bench corpus (real .map files) lives in fixtures/perf/, fetched via script (license-clean); excluded from crate publish.
  • --features simd opt-in; default build is portable scalar. no_std builds verified for smap-vlq/-codec in CI matrix.
  • Peak RSS measured with /usr/bin/time -l (macOS) / valgrind --tool=massif (Linux); PERF.md records env (CPU, rustc ver).
  • Profiling: cargo flamegraph on smap decode perf/synth_10m.map; hotspots expected in decode_vlq + segment push.

25. Threat Model

Posture. Every input to this toolkit is untrusted by default: a source map fetched over HTTP, embedded inline as a data: URL, or read from a build artifact written by another tool; the mappings/VLQ string; the v3 JSON envelope; the sourceMappingURL comment in code. Builder/edit inputs (positions emitted by a trusted local transpiler) are lower trust-but-verify, not trusted. The library is a codec + model, not a network or fetch agent — it never performs I/O on behalf of a map (no auto-fetch of sources/sourceMappingURL); the embedding application owns all fetching, sandboxing, and path-resolution policy. Goal: no panic, no UB, no unbounded resource use, no implicit I/O on any adversarial input.

25.1 Trust boundaries

            UNTRUSTED                          | TRUST-BUT-VERIFY      | TRUSTED
  HTTP / data: URL / build artifact            | local transpiler #264 | embedding app's
  ─ raw JSON bytes                             |   add_mapping(gen,orig)|   fetch/sandbox/
  ─ "mappings" / VLQ string                    |   add_source/add_name  |   path policy
  ─ //# sourceMappingURL= comment              |                        |
  ─ sourcesContent[], sources[], sourceRoot    |                        |
